ShiftedConditionStatement.shifted_condition_threshold_statement =
forall (C alpha : Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R)
  (_ : is_true
         (@preorder.Order.lt orderedzmod.ring_display
            (numdomain.Num.NumDomain.Exports.Num_NumDomain__to__Order_Preorder
               Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__Num_NumDomain)
            (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
               (numdomain.Num.NumDomain.Exports.join_Num_NumDomain_between_Order_Preorder_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
                  Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__Num_NumDomain))
            C))
  (_ : is_true
         (@preorder.Order.lt orderedzmod.ring_display
            (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_and_Order_Preorder
               Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__Num_POrderNmodule)
            (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
               (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.Num_POrderNmodule__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                  Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__Num_POrderNmodule))
            alpha))
  (n : forall _ : nat, nat)
  (m : forall d : nat, matrix.matrix Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R d (S O))
  (_ : UniversalityDefinitions.universality_density_tends_to n alpha)
  (_ : forall (eta : Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R)
         (_ : is_true
                (@preorder.Order.lt orderedzmod.ring_display
                   (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_and_Order_Preorder

                   (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
                      (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.Num_POrderNmodule__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                         Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__Num_POrderNmodule))
                   eta)),
       @ex nat
         (fun D : nat =>
          forall (d : nat) (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq D d)),
          is_true
            (@preorder.Order.le orderedzmod.ring_display
               (numfield.Num.RealClosedField.Exports.Num_RealClosedField__to__Order_Preorder
                  Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__Num_RealClosedField)
               (@euclidean.frobenius
                  Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__Num_RealClosedField
                  d (S O) (m d))
               (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
                  Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_PzSemiRing eta
                  (@numfield.Num.Def.sqrtr
                     Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__Num_RealClosedField
                     (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                        (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma

                              Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__Num_RealClosedField))
                        (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                           (numfield.Num.RealClosedField.Exports.Num_RealClosedField__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                              Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__Num_RealClosedField))
                        d)))))),
and
  (forall
     _ : is_true
           (@preorder.Order.lt orderedzmod.ring_display
              Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__Order_Preorder alpha
              (ConditionDefinitions.condition_alpha C)),
